### [阵-469] acad_lamp · 选项2 成功
【原文】
背面有字。不是手写——打字机打的，格式像公文。
焦痕只留了两行：

「……依据《城市心智存续计划》验收标准第七条，
本研究涉及受控信息，建议分级封存……」

你不认识「城市心智存续计划」。但你认识「验收」这个词。
验收的对象不是论文。

你把这两行抄下来，折好，放进口袋。
灯灭了——不是你关的，是电路保护，深夜自动断电。
你在黑暗里坐了一会儿。
【新文】
There is writing on the back. Not by hand—typed, laid out like an official document.
The scorching has left two lines:

“…pursuant to Acceptance Criterion Seven of the City Mind Continuity Program,
this research involves controlled information; classified sealing is recommended…”

You do not know the City Mind Continuity Program. But you know the word acceptance.
What was being accepted was not the paper.

You copy the two lines out, fold them, and put them in your pocket.
The lamp goes off—not by your hand. Circuit protection; the power cuts automatically late at night.
You sit in the dark a while.

### [阵-476] front_scales · 事件正文
【原文】
阵线的例会在一间租来的仓库里开。
墙上贴满了不同年份的标语。有两条挨着，互相矛盾——
左边「改不改是个人的事」，右边「个人的事就是所有人的事」。
两条都没人撕。

今天的议题只有一条：铁锤派昨夜又砸了一间诊所。阵线要不要公开谴责？

主持人站在折叠桌后面，桌上放着一只闹钟，每人发言限时三分钟。
闹钟是手动的，不含任何电子元件——这是阵线唯一一条没人反对过的规矩：
讨论改造权利的会上，器材一律原装。不是因为信仰，是为了不让任何一方觉得被冒犯。

你看着那只闹钟想：一个连闹钟都要斟酌的阵营，需要拍桌子的时候拍得下去吗？
【新文】
The Front’s regular meeting is held in a rented warehouse.
The walls are covered in slogans from different years. Two of them hang side by side and contradict each other—
on the left, “whether you augment is your own business,” on the right, “your own business is everybody’s business.”
Nobody has torn down either.

One item today: the Hammer smashed another clinic last night. Does the Front condemn it publicly?

The chair stands behind a folding table with an alarm clock on it; three minutes per speaker.
The clock is wind-up, with no electronics in it at all—the Front’s one rule nobody has ever objected to:
at a meeting about the right to augment, all equipment is original. Not out of belief. So that no side feels slighted.

You look at that clock and think: a faction that has to deliberate over an alarm clock—when it needs to bang the table, can it?

### [阵-477] front_scales · 选项1
【原文】
投谴责票。暴力就是暴力，不需要立场来定义。
【新文】
Vote to condemn. Violence is violence; it does not need a position to define it.

### [阵-478] front_scales · 选项1 成功
【原文】
你站起来的时候，主持人看了你一眼——不是惊讶，是确认。
像在心里划掉了一个名字旁边的问号。

「谴责暴力不等于选边。一个人有权选择不改造，
但没有人有权砸掉别人改造的地方。诊所没了，选择就没了。」

表决的时候你注意到一件事：投谴责票的人里有三个手上有旧伤——
工伤、烫伤、截指。投反对票的人，手都是完整的。
【新文】
As you stand, the chair looks at you—not surprised. Confirming.
As though crossing out a question mark beside a name.

“Condemning violence is not taking a side. A person has the right to choose not to augment,
but nobody has the right to smash the place where other people do. With the clinic gone, the choice is gone.”

During the vote you notice something: three of the people voting to condemn have old injuries on their hands—
work injury, burn, a missing finger. The people voting against have whole hands.

### [阵-479] front_scales · 选项1 失败
【原文】
你站起来想说话，闹钟响了。三分钟的规矩不看人。

You stand up to speak and the clock goes off. Three minutes is three minutes, whoever you are.
The chair gives you a look—sorry, but the rule is the rule.
You sit. Somebody beside you says quietly:
“Don’t take it to heart. Less changes in three minutes at this meeting than you think.”

### [阵-480] front_scales · 选项2

### [阵-481] front_scales · 选项2 成功
【原文】
「阵线的原则是选择权。铁锤派选择了暴力——那是他们的事。
我们的问题是：谴责之后怎么办？
谴责了铁锤，下次飞升螺旋逼人植芯片，我们也得谴责。再下一次，又是谁？
然后我们就不再是中间了，我们变成了仲裁者。
仲裁者需要权力。我们只有一间仓库。」

有人鼓掌了，稀疏的。不是因为你说得对，
是因为你替他们说出了一种他们需要、但说不出口的犹豫。
【新文】
“The Front’s principle is the right to choose. The Hammer chose violence—that is their business.
Our question is: what happens after we condemn?
Condemn the Hammer, and next time the Ascension Spiral forces a chip on somebody, we have to condemn that too. And after that, who?
And then we are not the middle anymore. We have become arbiters.
Arbiters need power. We have a warehouse.”

Somebody applauds, thinly. Not because you are right,
but because you said aloud a hesitation they needed and could not say.

### [阵-482] front_scales · 选项2 失败

你的发言引来连续三个人的反驳。他们说你在替暴力辩护。你没有。
但「不谴责」这个词在这间仓库里听起来就是「容忍」。
主持人让大家安静。
你坐着听——那些话都有道理，你的话也有道理。

### [阵-484] front_scales · 选项3 结果
【原文】
你没有举手。主持人记下了你的弃权——
弃权在这个阵营里不算态度，弃权是默认状态。
很多人是带着弃权来到平权阵线的：
他们在别的地方弃了权，然后走到了中间。

投票结果你没听清。你在看人。
投谴责票的人是站起来投的。投反对票的人举手——能少动就少动。
弃权的人什么都没做。

三种姿势。三种和信念的距离。
【新文】
You do not raise your hand. The chair records your abstention—
abstention is not a stance in this faction. Abstention is the default state.
A great many people arrive at the Parity Front already abstaining:
they gave up somewhere else, and then walked to the middle.

You do not catch the result. You are watching people.
Those voting to condemn stand up to vote. Those voting against raise a hand—as little movement as possible.
Those abstaining do nothing.

Three postures. Three distances from a conviction.

### [阵-485] front_scales · 回响1
【原文】
同一间仓库，同一只闹钟。墙上的标语多了一条，但你读不清——
有人把另一条贴在了它上面。也许不是反对，也许只是墙不够了。
【新文】
The same warehouse, the same clock. There is one more slogan on the wall, but you cannot read it—
somebody has stuck another one over it. Maybe not in opposition. Maybe there was simply no wall left.

### [阵-486] front_scales · 技能之声·逻辑
【原文】
【逻辑】八比七。你数了三遍。多数只比少数多一个人。在这么小的房间里，多数决和抛硬币差多少？
【新文】
LOGIC — Eight to seven. You counted three times. The majority is larger than the minority by one person. In a room this small, how far is majority rule from a coin flip?

### [阵-487] front_triage · 事件正文
【原文】
码头边的临时义诊点。一间仓库改的，每周开两天。
坐诊的人每次不一样——灰港退下来的外科医生，学院派的实习生，
不留名字的志愿者（来了就干活，干完就走）。

今天排了十七个人。义肢只剩五副：三副腿，一副手臂，一颗人工瓣膜。
灰港的货，二手，编号磨掉了，合金成色没问题。

分诊规则贴在墙上，一共三个版本，不同年份写的：
最早一版「按到达顺序」。中间一版「按医学紧急程度」。最新一版「由值班人员判断」。
三个版本都没有被划掉。

今天你帮忙分诊。翻到第三张表格时你停住了——
九岁男孩，先天心室中隔缺损，需要那颗唯一的瓣膜。
母亲填的表。地址栏写的是纯血誓约的社区。
【新文】
A temporary free clinic by the docks. Converted warehouse, open two days a week.
Whoever is on rotation is different every time—retired surgeons out of Ashport, Academy interns,
volunteers who leave no names (they turn up, they work, they go).

Seventeen people booked today. Five prosthetics left: three legs, one arm, one artificial valve.
Ashport stock, secondhand, numbers filed off, alloy sound.

The triage rules are on the wall in three versions, written in different years:
the earliest, “order of arrival.” The middle one, “medical urgency.” The newest, “at the discretion of the person on duty.”
None of the three has been struck out.

You are helping with triage today. At the third form you stop—
boy, nine years old, congenital ventricular septal defect, needs the one valve.
The form was filled in by his mother. The address is a Blood Covenant community.

### [阵-488] front_triage · 选项1
【原文】
按病情排序。心室缺损排第一——出身不是医学指标。
【新文】
Order by condition. The septal defect goes first—where you were born is not a medical indicator.

### [阵-489] front_triage · 选项1 成功
【原文】
你把十七份病历摊开。心室缺损排第一——不装瓣膜，活不过明年冬天。
值班医生没有看地址栏。也许看了。签字的时候没有犹豫。

手术在仓库后面的隔间里做的。二手瓣膜，编号磨掉了，尺寸刚好。
男孩醒来的时候叫了一声妈。母亲抱着他。
她的后背绣着纯血誓约的徽章。

一颗灰港的二手瓣膜，装在纯血社区孩子的心脏里，
在平权阵线的义诊点跳动。
【新文】
You lay the seventeen files out. The septal defect goes first—without the valve he does not get past next winter.
The doctor on duty does not look at the address field. Or perhaps she does. There is no hesitation when she signs.

The surgery is done in a partitioned room behind the warehouse. Secondhand valve, numbers filed off, exactly the right size.
When the boy wakes he calls for his mother. She holds him.
There is a Blood Covenant emblem embroidered on the back of her coat.

A secondhand Ashport valve, in the heart of a child from a pure-blood community,
beating in a Parity Front clinic.

### [阵-490] front_triage · 选项1 失败
【原文】
你排完序，值班医生改了一个位置——
把一个工伤的码头工人提到了男孩前面。
「码头工明天不装腿就丢饭碗。男孩的瓣膜能等三周——
下一批灰港的货里有心脏配件。」
你想反驳。但医生拿出了排期表：三周后有一批到港。
她在赌。赌灰港的船准时。

三周后船到了。男孩装上了瓣膜。
那三周你没睡好。
【新文】
You finish the ordering and the duty doctor moves one place—
she puts a dock worker with a work injury ahead of the boy.
“The dock worker loses his job tomorrow if he doesn’t get the leg. The boy’s valve can wait three weeks—
there are cardiac parts in the next Ashport shipment.”
You want to argue. But the doctor produces the schedule: a shipment lands in three weeks.
She is gambling. Gambling that Ashport’s boat is on time.

Three weeks later the boat arrives. The boy gets his valve.
You did not sleep well for those three weeks.

### [阵-491] front_triage · 选项2
【原文】
把地址指给值班医生看。值班的人应该知道全部信息。
【新文】
Point the address out to the duty doctor. The person on duty should have all the information.

### [阵-492] front_triage · 选项2 成功
【原文】
值班医生看了地址栏。沉默了五秒——
不是犹豫的五秒，是翻阅的五秒。她在翻自己的记忆。

「你知道这孩子为什么心室缺损？」
你说不知道。
「先天的，和改造没有任何关系。
但纯血的社区诊所不做心脏手术——心脏手术需要植入物，
植入物违反誓约。所以他们的孩子生下来心脏有问题，就只能——」

她没说完。她在病历上签了字。
「他是什么人家的孩子不重要。重要的是他的心脏缺一个零件，
而我们手上有这个零件。」
【新文】
The doctor looks at the address field. Five seconds of silence—
not five seconds of hesitation. Five seconds of looking something up. She is going through her own memory.

“Do you know why this child has a septal defect?”
You say you don’t.
“It’s congenital. It has nothing to do with augmentation at all.
But the pure-blood community clinics don’t do cardiac surgery—cardiac surgery requires an implant,
and implants violate the covenant. So when their children are born with something wrong with the heart, all they can—”

She does not finish. She signs the notes.
“Whose child he is does not matter. What matters is that his heart is short a part,
and we have that part in our hands.”

### [阵-493] front_triage · 选项2 失败
【原文】
值班医生听完，没有表情。
「你觉得我会因为地址栏拒绝一个孩子？」
你说不是这个意思。她说她知道。
但空气裂了一道缝——义诊点的「义」字不加脚注。
【新文】
The doctor hears you out with no expression at all.
“You think I would turn away a child over an address field?”
You say that was not what you meant. She says she knows.
But the air has a crack in it now—the *free* in free clinic takes no footnote.

### [阵-494] front_triage · 选项3

### [阵-495] front_triage · 选项3 结果
【原文】
母亲不识字。你帮她一栏一栏地填。
填到「过敏史」的时候她说：「青霉素。还有……」
她犹豫了一下：「他爸不让我来。」

你停了笔。
「他爸说，我们的孩子靠祷告活着。」
她低下头：「但我带他来了。我跟他爸说去买菜。」

你把「过敏史：青霉素」写完。没有写别的。
表格交给值班医生。医生看了一眼地址，看了一眼过敏史，签了字。
签字这个动作在义诊点每天发生四十次。这一次的笔画重一点。
也许是你的错觉。
【新文】
The mother cannot read. You fill it in for her, box by box.
At “allergy history” she says: “Penicillin. And…”
She hesitates: “His father wouldn’t let me come.”

Your pen stops.
“His father says our child lives by prayer.”
She lowers her head: “But I brought him. I told his father I was going for groceries.”

You finish writing “Allergy history: penicillin.” You do not write anything else.
The form goes to the duty doctor. She glances at the address, glances at the allergy history, signs.
Signing happens forty times a day at this clinic. This time the strokes are a little heavier.
Perhaps you imagined it.

### [阵-501] front_triage · 技能之声·共情
【原文】
【共情】十七个人，五副义肢。你在做的事有一个哲学名字，叫分配正义。
哲学课不讲的是：做完之后有十二个人要走回家，而他们的腿还是少一条。
【新文】
EMPATHY — Seventeen people, five prosthetics. What you are doing has a name in philosophy: distributive justice.
What the philosophy course leaves out is that afterward twelve people walk home, and they are still short a leg.

### [阵-502] front_scar · 事件正文
【原文】
义诊结束后收拾场地。你在叠折叠椅。
旁边有个人也在叠——动作很熟练，像是每周都在做这件事。

她的袖口在搬椅子的时候滑上去了。
左手腕上一道旧疤，灼痕。边缘早就平了，颜色比周围浅一号。
你见过这种疤。圣殿派入教仪式留下的。

她注意到你在看。没有遮，把袖口卷上去，继续搬椅子。
灼痕旁边是更新的痕迹——针眼、消毒水的灼红、搬折叠桌磨出来的茧。
旧疤是她身上唯一一道不是自己选的。
【新文】
Clearing up after the clinic session. You are stacking folding chairs.
Somebody beside you is stacking too—practiced about it, like a person who does this every week.

Her cuff slides up as she lifts a chair.
On the left wrist there is an old scar, a burn. The edges went smooth long ago; the color is a shade lighter than the skin around it.
You have seen that scar. It is what the Temple’s initiation leaves.

She notices you looking. She does not cover it; she rolls the cuff further up and goes on with the chairs.
Beside the burn there are newer marks—needle points, the red of disinfectant, calluses worn on from carrying folding tables.
The old scar is the only mark on her she did not choose.

### [阵-503] front_scar · 选项1
【原文】
「你以前是圣殿的人？」
【新文】
“You used to be Temple?”

### [阵-504] front_scar · 选项1 成功
【原文】
她把最后一把椅子靠在墙边，坐下来。
不是准备讲故事的坐法，是干活干累了的坐法。

「十二岁入的教。你知道圣殿的烫法——每个孩子一道，
代表『我选择完整的身体』。十二岁的时候我觉得这句话很漂亮。」

她看着自己的手腕。
「后来我邻居家有个女孩，七岁，先天瓣膜缺损。
社区里所有人都说『我们的孩子不需要金属活着』。
她需要。但没有人帮她装——不是因为没有瓣膜，是因为共识。
共识不看心电图。」

她没有继续说。你也没有问后来怎么样了。
如果女孩活下来了，她今天不会在这里叠椅子。

「没有人杀她。」她的声音很平。「是所有人的沉默杀了她。
沉默有体量。」
【新文】
She sets the last chair against the wall and sits down.
Not the way you sit to tell a story. The way you sit when the work has tired you.

“Joined at twelve. You know how the Temple burns you—one on every child,
for *I choose a body that is whole*. At twelve I thought that sentence was beautiful.”

She looks at her own wrist.
“Later there was a girl next door. Seven, born with a valve defect.
Everyone in the community said *our children don’t need metal to live.*
She did. But nobody would fit it—not because there was no valve. Because of the consensus.
A consensus does not read an ECG.”

She does not go on. You do not ask what happened.
If the girl had lived, she would not be here stacking chairs today.

“Nobody killed her.” Her voice is level. “The silence of everybody killed her.
Silence has mass.”

### [阵-505] front_scar · 选项1 失败
【原文】
「以前是。」她说完这两个字就站起来继续搬桌子了。
你帮她抬了一头。桌子很重——器械箱还压在上面。
搬到角落，她说了一句「谢谢」。
那个「谢谢」不是在回答你。
但它准确地告诉了你一件事：今天她只想搬完桌子回家。
【新文】
“I was.” Two words, and she is up and moving tables again.
You take one end for her. The table is heavy—the instrument case is still on it.
When it is in the corner she says thank you.
The thank you was not an answer to your question.
But it tells you one thing exactly: today she only wants to finish the tables and go home.

### [阵-506] front_scar · 选项2
【原文】
「为什么没去掉那个疤？」
【新文】
“Why didn’t you have the scar taken off?”

### [阵-507] front_scar · 选项2 成功
【原文】
她看了一眼手腕。「因为它是真的。」

你等她解释。她想了想。
「我在圣殿待过，这是事实。我离开了，这也是事实。
这道疤不矛盾。它同时装着这两件事。」

她又想了想。「你知道这个阵营里的人怎么看这道疤？
他们看见勇气——她离开了另一边。
圣殿的人怎么看？他们看见背叛。同一道疤，两种翻译。」

她把袖口放下来。
「去掉了就只剩『她走了』。留着，还剩『她来过』。」
【新文】
She glances at the wrist. “Because it is true.”

You wait for the rest. She thinks about it.
“I was in the Temple. That is a fact. I left. That is also a fact.
This scar is not a contradiction. It holds both of them at once.”

She thinks a little more. “Do you know how people in this faction read this scar?
They see courage—she left the other side.
And how the Temple reads it? They see betrayal. One scar, two translations.”

She pulls the cuff back down.
“Take it off and all that is left is *she went.* Keep it and there is still *she was there.*”

### [阵-508] front_scar · 选项2 失败

你分不清这是玩笑还是真话。
【新文】
“Take it off and I lose a conversation starter.” She smiles a little.
You cannot tell whether that was a joke or the truth.

### [阵-509] front_scar · 选项3

【新文】

### [阵-510] front_scar · 选项3 结果
【原文】
你没开口。她也没说话。
你们叠了十四把椅子。七把一摞，两摞，靠墙。

她叠椅子的方式很圣殿——每把椅子对齐，椅腿之间的间距一致，
像抄经时的行距。有些习惯比信仰活得久。

叠完她哼了一段曲子。你听不出旋律，
但节奏像圣殿晚祷的格律，只是调子改了。

她走的时候说：「下周见。」
不是客套。是一个每周都来叠椅子的人，
对另一个每周都来的人的确认。
【新文】
You do not speak. Neither does she.
You stack fourteen chairs. Seven to a stack, two stacks, against the wall.

The way she stacks them is very Temple—every chair aligned, the gaps between the legs even,
like the line spacing when you copy scripture. Some habits outlive the faith.

When she is finished she hums a few bars. You cannot place the melody,
but the meter runs like the Temple’s evening prayer, only in a different key.

On the way out she says: “See you next week.”
Not a pleasantry. A person who comes every week to stack chairs,
confirming it with another person who comes every week.

### [阵-511] front_scar · 回响1
【原文】
你在圣殿区路过一扇门。门上挂着旧了的祈祷花环，干枯了，没人取下来。
门牌号码你看了一眼就记住了。你不知道为什么。
【新文】
You pass a door in the Temple district. There is an old prayer wreath on it, dried out, that nobody has taken down.
You glance at the house number and it stays with you. You do not know why.

### [阵-512] front_scar · 回响2
【原文】
圣殿的晚祷上有人在哼一段旋律。你听过——
在义诊点收拾场地的时候，另一个人用不同的调子哼过它。
同一首歌，两个版本。你开始不确定哪个是原版。
【新文】
Somebody is humming a melody at the Temple’s evening prayer. You have heard it—
somebody else hummed it in a different key while clearing up at the free clinic.
The same song, two versions. You are beginning to be unsure which is the original.

### [阵-513] front_wall · 事件正文
【原文】
清晨。你到仓库的时候，发现墙上多了东西。

左墙——喷漆，字迹潦草，笔画有力：
「你们的义诊点就是一间改造工厂」
没有落款。但那种暴烈的写法你见过，铁锤派的人这样写字。

右墙——粘贴的打印纸，排版整齐，每个字都居中：
「中间路线是谎言。不全改就是半个人。」
这种干净的排版不像街头，更像实验室。

仓库门没有锁——阵线从来不锁门，「我们的门对所有人开着」是原则。
今天你看着这两面墙想：门对所有人开着，包括来砸门的人。

药柜被翻了。不知道是谁翻的。也许是左边来的，也许是右边来的，
也许是个普通小偷。中间地带的敌人没有统一制服。
【新文】
Early morning. When you get to the warehouse there is something new on the walls.

Left wall—spray paint, scrawled, the strokes hard:
“YOUR FREE CLINIC IS AN AUGMENTATION FACTORY”
No signature. But you have seen that violent hand before. Hammer people write like that.

Right wall—printed sheets pasted up, neatly typeset, every line centered:
“THE MIDDLE ROAD IS A LIE. ANYTHING SHORT OF ALL OF IT IS HALF A PERSON.”
Typesetting that clean is not the street. It is more like a laboratory.

The warehouse door is not locked—the Front never locks it. *Our door is open to everyone* is a principle.
Today you look at these two walls and think: open to everyone, including the people who come to break it down.

The medicine cabinet has been gone through. Nobody knows by whom. Maybe somebody from the left, maybe from the right,
maybe an ordinary thief. The enemies of the middle ground do not come in a single uniform.

### [阵-514] front_wall · 选项1
【原文】
两面墙都刷掉。不给任何一方留话柄。
【新文】
Paint both walls out. Give neither side anything to point at.

### [阵-515] front_wall · 选项1 成功
【原文】
你找到一桶白漆。不是全新的——上次刷墙剩的，桶底还有半指深。
先刷左墙。红漆很厚，字迹在白色底下隐约透出来，像旧伤上的新皮。
刷了三遍才彻底盖住。

然后刷右墙。打印纸撕下来的时候，胶带带走了一小块墙皮。
白漆补上去，留了一个凹坑。

两面墙刷完，仓库比以前更白。白到不自然。
有人走进来看了一眼，说：「又刷了？」
「又」这个字告诉你：这不是第一次。
【新文】
You find a can of white paint. Not new—left over from the last time the walls were done, half a finger deep at the bottom.
The left wall first. The red is thick, and the strokes show faintly through the white, like new skin over an old wound.
It takes three coats to bury it.

Then the right wall. When the printed sheets come off, the tape takes a patch of plaster with it.
The white paint goes over it and leaves a dent.

Both walls done, the warehouse is whiter than it was. Unnaturally white.
Somebody walks in, looks around, and says: “Painted again?”
That *again* tells you: this is not the first time.

### [阵-516] front_wall · 选项1 失败

但你听见有人小声说：「他只刷了铁锤那边。」
【新文】
There is not enough paint. You finish the left wall and get halfway across the right.
Half white and half writing looks worse than leaving all of it.
The people coming to the meeting that afternoon ask why you only did one side. You say the paint ran out.
But you hear somebody say quietly: “He only painted out the Hammer’s side.”

### [阵-518] front_wall · 选项2 成功

You do nothing. The people arrive for the meeting and see the two walls.
Somebody reads the left, turns and reads the right, then stands in the middle and turns his head twice—
like watching tennis.

“No need to look up today’s agenda,” you say. “The agenda is on the walls.”

### [阵-519] front_wall · 选项2 失败

You leave both walls. But nobody mentions them at the meeting. Everybody pretends not to see.

They have learned to live in the same warehouse as the writing on the walls.

### [阵-521] front_wall · 选项3 结果
【原文】
你站在空白前面，手里有半桶白漆和一支旧刷子。
左边说你是改造工厂。右边说你是绊脚石。
你想回答。但中间的回答长什么样？

你举起刷子，想了很久。最后写下一行：

「这面墙两边都漏风。」

不是口号，不是辩护，是一个事实——仓库确实漏风，
冬天的风从两边灌进来，两面墙都挡不住。

但你还是写了。因为两个极端都有现成的句子可以引用，
圣殿有经文，飞升有定理。中间没有。
中间只有你此刻想到的这一句。
【新文】
You stand in front of the blank with half a can of white paint and an old brush.
The left says you are an augmentation factory. The right says you are an obstacle.
You want to answer. But what does an answer from the middle look like?

You raise the brush and think for a long time. In the end you write one line:

“THIS WALL LEAKS FROM BOTH SIDES.”

Not a slogan, not a defense. A fact—the warehouse does leak,
winter wind comes in from both sides and neither wall keeps it out.

You write it anyway. Because both extremes have ready-made sentences to quote:
the Temple has scripture, ascension has theorems. The middle has none.
The middle has only the one sentence you thought of just now.

### [阵-525] front_pendulum · 事件正文
【原文】
夜。例会散了，所有人都走了。你没走。

仓库很空。折叠椅靠在墙边。
天花板上一盏灯挂在电线上，风从破窗灌进来，灯在晃。
不快不慢，像一只倒过来的钟摆。

你坐在地上看它晃。

墙上有两行字。不是外面喷的，是阵线自己人在不同的会上写的：

「改不改身体是个人的事。」——去年三月
「个人的事从来就不只是个人的事。」——今年一月

两句都是这个阵营说的。两句都对。两句互相否定。
【新文】
Night. The meeting is over and everyone has gone. You have not.

The warehouse is empty. Folding chairs against the wall.
A lamp hangs from a wire in the ceiling, and the wind comes in through the broken window, and the lamp swings.
Not fast, not slow, like a pendulum hung upside down.

You sit on the floor and watch it swing.

There are two lines on the wall. Not sprayed from outside—written by the Front’s own people, at different meetings:

“Whether you change your body is your own business.” —March, last year
“Your own business has never been only your own.” —January, this year

Both said by this faction. Both true. Each negating the other.

### [阵-527] front_pendulum · 选项1 结果

灯还在晃。如果它停下来，会停在哪一边？
物理学说：正中间。
但那是因为没有人在推它。
【新文】
You think of the day of the vote. Eight to seven.
Of the clinic’s triage rules—three versions, not one struck out.
Of this faction’s alarm clock—wind-up, because even the timekeeping must not offend either side.

Perhaps the middle is not a conviction. The middle is insurance.
If the pure bloods turn out to be right, at least you have not changed much and can still go back.
If ascension turns out to be right, at least you did not refuse, and you are not left behind.

The lamp is still swinging. If it stopped, which side would it stop on?
Physics says: dead center.
But that is because nobody is pushing it.

### [阵-529] front_pendulum · 选项2 结果
【原文】
两个极端都很确定。确定是一种奢侈品——
确定意味着你不需要再问了。
纯血确定身体不可改，依据是信仰。
飞升确定身体应该全改，依据是效率。
信仰不需要证据，效率不需要同意。两种确定都不需要对方。

中间需要对方。中间要同时容纳两种可能——
不是因为它们都对，是因为你不知道哪个对。
而「不知道」在这个世界上是最不受欢迎的立场：
没有旗帜，没有经文，没有可以引用的句子。

灯还在晃。
也许摆的意义不在于停在哪里，而在于它还在晃。
【新文】
Both extremes are certain. Certainty is a luxury good—
certainty means you no longer have to ask.
The pure bloods are certain the body cannot be altered, on the grounds of faith.
Ascension is certain the body should be altered entirely, on the grounds of efficiency.
Faith needs no evidence; efficiency needs no consent. Neither certainty needs the other side.

The middle needs the other side. The middle has to hold two possibilities at once—
not because both are right, but because you do not know which is.
And *not knowing* is the least popular position in this world:
no flag, no scripture, no sentence anyone can quote.

The lamp is still swinging.
Perhaps the meaning of a pendulum is not where it stops, but that it is still swinging.

### [阵-531] front_pendulum · 选项3 结果
【原文】
风从破窗进来，从没关好的门出去。
你坐在折叠椅组成的阴影里。

你不想了。不是因为想通了，
是因为有些问题不是用想的，是用坐的。

灯还在晃。你注意到一件事：
灯的影子在地上画圆弧，来回，来回。
弧线的两端是那两行字。影子每次都经过两端，但不停留。
它唯一停留的地方是正中间——但只有一瞬，最快的那一瞬。

经过中间的速度最快。停在中间的时间最短。
你可以经过中间。你不能住在中间。
【新文】
The wind comes in through the broken window and goes out through the door that never shuts properly.
You sit in the shadow the folding chairs make.

You stop thinking. Not because you have worked it out,
but because some questions are not worked out by thinking. They are worked out by sitting.

The lamp is still swinging. You notice something:
the lamp’s shadow draws an arc on the floor, back, and back again.
The two ends of the arc are those two lines of writing. The shadow passes both ends every time and stops at neither.
The only place it stops is dead center—and only for an instant, the fastest instant there is.

Passing through the middle is the fastest. Stopping in the middle is the shortest.
You can pass through the middle. You cannot live in it.

### [阵-535] swarm_sync · 事件正文
【原文】
合流试运行之后的第三周。群智派给你安排了一对一校准——正式编入节点前的最后一步。

房间很小。两把椅子，一根线缆，一盏灯。
你的校准搭档坐在对面，义眼虹膜是旧型号的铜色。她在合流里待了十一年。

「校准不是考试。」她把线缆递给你，「你不需要告诉我任何事。
想一件东西，让线缆把形状传过来。不是内容——是形状。」

她每个词的重量一样。十一年的共享思维磨掉了某些东西——不是情感，是着重号。
【新文】
The third week after the trial run of the confluence. The Swarm has scheduled you a one-to-one calibration—the last step before formal enrollment as a node.

The room is small. Two chairs, one cable, one lamp.
Your calibration partner sits opposite; the iris of her prosthetic eye is an older model, copper-colored. She has been in the confluence eleven years.

“Calibration is not an examination.” She hands you the cable. “You do not have to tell me anything.
Think of one thing and let the cable carry the shape across. Not the content—the shape.”

Every word she says weighs the same. Eleven years of shared thinking has worn something away—not feeling. Emphasis.

### [阵-537] swarm_sync · 选项1 成功
【原文】
没有画面，没有声音。

先是温度——不是皮肤的温度，是思维的温度。
她的意识比你预想的凉。不是冷。是深水的那种静。

然后你感觉到了自己。不是照镜子——是从她的位置看过来的你。
你的思维是热的，边缘毛糙，形状不规则。
你第一次知道自己想东西的时候长什么样。

【新文】
No image, no sound.

First there is temperature—not the temperature of skin. The temperature of thinking.
Her mind is cooler than you expected. Not cold. The stillness of deep water.

Then you feel yourself. Not like a mirror—like yourself seen from where she is.
Your thinking is hot, ragged at the edges, irregular in shape.
For the first time you know what you look like while you are thinking.

Twenty minutes. Afterward she unplugs the cable: “You are very noisy. That is not a bad thing.”

你只听。

You only listen.

Eleven years of confluence have ground her mind to a texture you have not met—
every boundary confirmed and reconfirmed until it is transparent.
Like a polished piece of glass: you cannot see it, but light changes angle going through.

Calibration ends. She unplugs the cable: “You gave nothing.”

You say that is right.

“That is allowed. But you should know—people who only listen and never give have a name in the confluence.”

She does not tell you the name.

### [阵-547] swarm_ghost · 事件正文
【原文】
紧急会议。合流里一位成员的肉身脑死亡已经十九天。生命维持还在运转。

但她的节点还活着。不是缓存——是在运算。
十九天里，这个节点回应了三十七次会议投票、两次技术咨询，
还替一位新成员做了校准。合流说它的响应模式和脑死亡前完全一致。

家属来了。她丈夫坐在会议室角落，手里攥着一份司法鉴定书：脑死亡，不可逆。

导师打开频谱图。屏幕上，她的节点稳定地闪烁——和其余节点完全同步。

「合流认为她在。」导师说。
丈夫抬头：「她不在。」
【新文】
Emergency meeting. A member’s body has been brain-dead for nineteen days. Life support is still running.

But her node is alive. Not cached—computing.
In nineteen days that node has answered thirty-seven meeting votes, given two technical consultations,
and run a calibration for a new member. The confluence says its response pattern is identical to before the brain death.

The family has come. Her husband sits in the corner of the meeting room holding a forensic certificate: brain death, irreversible.

The mentor brings up the spectrogram. On the screen her node blinks steadily—in complete synchrony with the rest.

“The confluence holds that she is present,” the mentor says.
The husband looks up: “She is not present.”

节点关闭的瞬间，合流里其余节点的频率抖了一下——
像一张网被剪掉了一根线。
【新文】
You stand.

“In nineteen days that node has cast thirty-seven votes.
But her voting record in life—she hesitated six to eight seconds before every vote.
The node has not hesitated once.”

You put the data up. The mentor stares at the screen for a long time.

“Hesitation is not latency. Hesitation is a person weighing something.
A node that does not hesitate is executing—it is computing in her pattern,
but there is nobody inside the computation doing the weighing.”

The husband signs the shutdown agreement.
In the instant the node closes, the frequency of every other node in the confluence shivers—
like a net with one thread cut out of it.

### [阵-561] swarm_count · 事件正文
【原文】
年度清点。每年一次，合流全网做一遍完整的节点核查。

你被分到第三扇区——一百四十二个注册节点，
逐一核对身份签名、活跃状态、带宽占用。枯燥的活。

第一遍清点，一百四十二个节点全部在线。你签字，准备提交。

然后你看见了总表。三个扇区加起来，注册节点四百一十七个。
活跃信号四百三十一个。

多了十四个。

「是回声。」导师扫了一眼，「深度合流会留下残余信号。每年都有几个，修一修就没了。」

你看着那十四个信号的频谱。不像回声——回声会衰减。
这些信号的振幅稳定，带宽占用恒定。
【新文】
The annual count. Once a year the whole confluence network runs a complete node audit.

You are assigned sector three—one hundred and forty-two registered nodes,
identity signature, activity status and bandwidth usage checked one at a time. Dull work.

First pass: all one hundred and forty-two are online. You sign off and prepare to submit.

Then you see the master sheet. Across three sectors, four hundred and seventeen registered nodes.
Four hundred and thirty-one active signals.

Fourteen extra.

“Echoes,” the mentor says with a glance. “Deep confluence leaves residual signals. There are a few every year; a little maintenance and they’re gone.”

You look at the spectra of those fourteen. They do not look like echoes—echoes decay.
The amplitude of these is stable and their bandwidth usage constant.

全体表决。议题：改革合流的投票权重。

共振频率越高，你的一票越重。最重的节点一票顶十二票。

【新文】
A full vote. The motion: reform the voting weights of the confluence.

The current rule allocates weight by depth of fusion—the longer you have been in the confluence, the deeper your connection
and the higher your resonance frequency, the heavier your vote. The heaviest node’s vote is worth twelve.

Today’s motion: one node, one vote, regardless of fusion.

The person who filed the motion is the lightest node on the network—calibrated less than six months ago.
Under the current rule, his vote is worth zero point three.

Method of voting: the current rule. Unequal weights, used to decide whether to be equal.

“You are measuring whether you are crooked with a crooked ruler.”

The room goes quiet.

“Voting under the current weights, the chance of passage is under three percent—
the twelve heaviest nodes hold forty percent of the total. This vote has only two possible outcomes:
it fails, which shows the rule protected itself; or it passes, which means the heaviest nodes gave up their privilege voluntarily—and that takes a miracle.”

The mentor writes in her notebook for a long time. Finally she looks up: “You have not said what to do.”

“Say the contradiction out loud first. Until it is said, whatever we vote on is scratching graduations onto a crooked ruler.”

The vote is postponed. The postponement was itself decided by a vote under the current weights.

### [阵-587] swarm_floor · 事件正文
【原文】
深夜。你在合流的同步舱里做例行维护——
清理缓存、校验接口、检查带宽分配。

这个时间合流几乎静默。四百多个节点只有不到二十个还醒着。
网络本底应该是一片均匀的灰。

你切到底层频段做噪声扫描。

不均匀。

灰里有一条线。不是干扰——干扰是尖的，这条线很平，振幅恒定，频率精确。
你放大了看：不是一条线，是一组信号。编码方式不是当前合流协议。

你查了合流的版本历史。这组信号的编码比合流最早的版本还要旧。
【新文】
Late at night. You are doing routine maintenance in the confluence’s sync pod—
clearing caches, verifying interfaces, checking bandwidth allocation.

At this hour the confluence is nearly silent. Fewer than twenty of the four hundred-odd nodes are awake.
The network floor should be an even grey.

You switch to the bottom band for a noise sweep.

It is not even.

There is a line in the grey. Not interference—interference is spiky. This line is flat, its amplitude constant, its frequency exact.
You magnify it: not one line. A group of signals. The encoding is not the current confluence protocol.

You check the confluence’s version history. This encoding is older than the earliest version of the confluence.

顺着信号往上游追。它从哪来，通向哪里。
【新文】

### [阵-589] swarm_floor · 选项1 成功
【原文】
你花了四十分钟沿着信号走。
它不在合流的网络拓扑图上——它在拓扑图的下面。像地板下面的管道。

信号通向一个你从未见过的地址空间。地址格式和合流完全不同。
你进不去。但你在入口读到了一行元数据：

「存续节点 #0。状态：监听。权限：只读。」

你把地址抄下来。退出维护模式。
同步舱的灯在你退出的时候闪了一下。
【新文】
You spend forty minutes walking along the signal.
It is not on the confluence’s network topology—it is underneath the topology. Like pipes under a floor.

The signal leads to an address space you have never seen. The address format is nothing like the confluence’s.
You cannot get in. But at the entrance you read one line of metadata:

“Continuity node #0. Status: listening. Permission: read-only.”

You copy the address down and leave maintenance mode.
The sync pod’s light flickers once as you go.

信号从墙的另一边传过来。稳定、均匀、不理会你。

【新文】
You follow the signal for ten minutes and come to a wall.
Not a physical wall—a permission boundary. Your maintenance privileges end here.

The signal comes through from the other side. Steady, even, paying you no attention.

You back out. The maintenance log is automatically annotated: “Out-of-bounds access attempt recorded.”

### [阵-592] swarm_floor · 选项2 成功

群智派接手的时候，底层已经在运行了。

「基底协议不可修改。原因参见《城市心智存续计划》技术附件。」

你翻遍了整个存档。技术附件不在这里。
【新文】
The architecture documents are in the seventh archive layer. You go through them for three hours.
Most of it is technical specification—bandwidth allocation, node ceilings, protocol versions.

At the very bottom you find an unnumbered appendix. The title is two words: “Base Protocol.”

The content is short: the lower layer of the confluence network was not built by the Swarm.
When the Swarm took it over, the lower layer was already running.
The authoring body field of the base protocol is blank. The date field says “prior to this network.”

The last line of the appendix:
“The base protocol cannot be modified. For the reason, see the technical annex to the City Mind Continuity Program.”

You go through the entire archive. The technical annex is not here.

### [阵-595] swarm_floor · 选项3 结果
【原文】
你关了频谱图。底噪回到灰色。

退出同步舱的时候走廊很安静。
你的脚步声在金属地板上响了很久。

走了二十步之后你停下来。不是因为什么声音——是因为没有声音。
同步舱关门之后，你的脑子里应该是安静的。

【新文】
You close the spectrogram. The floor noise goes back to grey.

The corridor is very quiet as you come out of the sync pod.
Your footsteps sound on the metal floor for a long time.

Twenty paces on, you stop. Not because of a sound—because of the absence of one.
Once the sync pod door closes, it should be quiet inside your head.

It is not quiet. The floor noise is still there. Not the pod’s floor noise—your own.
You have been in the confluence too long. The floor noise has moved into your baseline.

### [阵-599] seed_compress · 事件正文
【原文】
压缩实验室在地下二层。墙上刷着一行字：「每一克带宽都是信仰。」

志愿者坐在连接椅上，脑后的接口亮着蓝光。她等这个位子等了七年。
今天是压缩测试——把一整个人的心智塞进探针的存储舱。

算法跑到百分之九十七卡住了。剩下的百分之三——技术员说大约是一段童年、
半套手艺、和某种气味的关联——与其余部分缠得太深，强行裁剪会损坏周围的记忆网络。

「三种裁法。」技术员把代价逐项说完，终于抬起头，却没有看你。

「决定权在她。我们只负责执行。」

志愿者望向你。她的手没有抖。

「帮我把代价说清楚。」她说，「别替我挑。」
【新文】
The compression lab is two floors down. A line is painted on the wall: “Every gram of bandwidth is faith.”

The volunteer sits in the connection chair, the interface behind her head lit blue. She waited seven years for this seat.
Today is the compression test—fitting an entire mind into the probe’s storage bay.

The algorithm stalls at ninety-seven percent. The remaining three percent—the technician says it is roughly a childhood,
half a craft, and the association of a certain smell—is too deeply tangled with the rest; forcing a cut would damage the surrounding memory network.

“Three ways to cut.” The technician goes through the costs one by one, then looks up, and not at you.

“The decision is hers. We only execute.”

The volunteer looks at you. Her hands are not shaking.

“Help me get the costs straight,” she says. “Don’t choose for me.”

【新文】
Explain first what losing the skill layer means; then, if she wants it, help her confirm the cut boundary.

### [阵-601] seed_compress · 选项1 成功

知道技能层和程序记忆之间的接缝在哪里。

裁剪干净。她的手艺从副本里消失了。

压缩完成。百分之百。她从椅子上站起来的时候说：
「到了之后我第一件事就是学修东西。」

你没有告诉她——重新学的手艺和原来的不一样。
用同一双手，走不同的弯路，犯不同的错误。
【新文】
You check the cut parameters beside her—you have done work like this,
and you know where the seam runs between the skill layer and procedural memory.

The cut is clean. Her craft is gone from the copy.

Compression completes. One hundred percent. Getting up out of the chair she says:
“First thing I do when I get there is learn to fix things.”

You do not tell her—a craft relearned is not the same craft.
The same pair of hands, taking different wrong turns, making different mistakes.

The skill layer and procedural memory are tangled tighter than you expected.
Your cut lands half a millimeter off—the skill comes out and takes a small piece with it that should have stayed.

The technician runs verification twice: “Core personality unaffected.
But when she gets there—she may not quite remember how to tie a shoelace.”

She hears it out from the chair. Then she says: “Then I’ll wear shoes that don’t need tying.”

### [阵-604] seed_compress · 选项2 成功

三年后探针传回第一批数据。其中有一段文字记录：

我发明了一个词来描述这种缺——『空鼻子』。这个词很蠢。但我每天都用。」
【新文】
The sensory mapping cuts more easily than the skill layer. Smell carries a surprisingly high weight in the memory network—
but it has few connection points, like a single independent root.

You confirm the cut boundary for the technician. One clean stroke.

Three years later the probe sends back its first data. Among it is a written record:
“There is something in the air. The sensors tell me the composition, but I do not know what it smells like.
I invented a word for this lack—*empty-nose.* It is a stupid word. I use it every day.”

### [阵-612] seed_farewell · 事件正文
【原文】
发射日。

探针固定在发射架上，外壳已经密封。志愿者——原本——站在观测台上。
探针里的副本已经通电。

播种者的规矩：发射前十二分钟，让副本醒来。原本和副本之间有一道玻璃。
十二分钟之后探针发射，此生再无交集。

你是今天的送行官。你站在两人之间，手里拿着倒计时器。

副本醒了。她眨了眨眼——和原本一模一样的眨法。
然后她看见了玻璃另一边的自己。

原本先开口了：「你怕吗？」
副本想了想：「你呢？」
【新文】
Launch day.

The probe is fixed to the gantry and the shell is sealed. The volunteer—the original—stands on the observation deck.
The copy inside the probe is already powered.

The Sowers’ rule: twelve minutes before launch, wake the copy. There is a pane of glass between original and copy.
Twelve minutes later the probe goes up, and they never meet again.

You are today’s send-off officer. You stand between them with the countdown in your hand.

The copy wakes. She blinks—exactly the way the original blinks.
Then she sees herself on the other side of the glass.

The original speaks first: “Are you afraid?”
The copy thinks about it: “Are you?”

### [阵-613] seed_farewell · 选项1

安静地守着。送行官的职责是在场，不是说话。
【新文】

### [阵-614] seed_farewell · 选项1 成功

副本的第一次哭和原本的第一次哭一模一样——同一个表情，同一个擦泪的动作。
原本的第二次哭，是看见副本复刻了自己的哭法之后。

倒计时器响了。你举起手。原本背过身去。副本没有。

探针发射的光照亮了观测台。
原本的影子被拉得很长，朝着探针飞走的反方向。
【新文】
Twelve minutes. You do not say one word.
The light of the launch fills the observation deck.
The original’s shadow stretches out long, pointing the opposite way from the probe.

### [阵-615] seed_farewell · 选项1 失败

### [阵-617] seed_farewell · 选项2 成功
【原文】
你把嘴凑近玻璃：「她想问你——到了之后，你还会想她吗？」

副本看了原本一眼。原本没有点头也没有摇头。

「我带着她所有的记忆走。」副本说，「所以我会想。
但想的是出发前的她。到了之后她又过了几十年——那个她，我不认识了。」

原本笑了一下。很短。
【新文】
You put your mouth close to the glass: “She wants to ask—when you get there, will you still think of her?”

The copy looks at the original. The original neither nods nor shakes her head.

“I am leaving with all of her memories,” the copy says. “So I will think of her.
But the her I think of is the one before departure. By the time I arrive she will have lived another few decades—that one, I do not know.”

The original smiles a little. Very briefly.

【原文】
你打开校验终端。十二分钟够跑一次快速诊断。

第七分钟你发现了一个问题：导航模块的零点校准有微小偏移。
不影响发射，但会在四十年的飞行中累积——到达时误差大约三百公里。
三百公里，在一颗没有地标的星球上，
可能是什么都没有和唯一一片适合着陆的地方之间的区别。

你修了。修好之后抬起头——最后两分钟，
原本和副本隔着玻璃把手贴在一起。
你看着她们的手。一模一样的掌纹。
【新文】
You open the verification terminal. Twelve minutes is enough for a fast diagnostic.

In the seventh minute you find a problem: a small offset in the navigation module’s zero calibration.
It will not affect the launch, but it will accumulate over forty years of flight—about three hundred kilometers of error on arrival.
Three hundred kilometers, on a planet with no landmarks,
may be the difference between nothing at all and the one place it is possible to land.

You fix it. When you look up—the last two minutes,
the original and the copy have their hands pressed together through the glass.
You look at their hands. Identical palm prints.

【新文】

### [阵-625] seed_return · 事件正文
【原文】
信号在凌晨三点到达。

探针「第七粒种子」，发射至今十七年，载着一位志愿者的心智副本飞向半人马座方向。
通讯窗口每十九个月开一次——这是第六次。前五次都是标准状态报告。

这一次多了一段个人信息。收件人写着原本的名字。

原本三年前死了。心脏——原装的那颗。他拒绝换。

信息很短。副本说他做了一个梦。
他知道副本不应该做梦——存储里没有给梦分配带宽。但他做了。
梦里他闻到了清汤面的味道。他问原本：你还记得那碗面吗？

你是值班接线员。收件人已经不在了。
【新文】
The signal arrives at three in the morning.

Probe *Seventh Seed*, seventeen years out, carrying a volunteer’s mind copy toward Centauri.
The communication window opens every nineteen months—this is the sixth. The first five were standard status reports.

This one has a personal message attached. The addressee is the original’s name.

The original died three years ago. His heart—the one he was born with. He refused to replace it.

The message is short. The copy says he had a dream.
He knows a copy is not supposed to dream—no bandwidth was allocated to dreams in storage. But he did.
In the dream he smelled clear-broth noodles. He asks the original: do you still remember that bowl of noodles?

You are the operator on duty. The addressee is no longer there.

She is quiet a moment: “Can I write back?”

You say she can. The next communication window is in nineteen months. She says there is time.

### [阵-638] seed_quiet · 事件正文
【原文】
「第三粒种子」失联两年零四天。

探针的硬件诊断每季度自动回传一次。硬件全部正常——
电力、推进、存储、通讯模块。通讯没坏。它只是不说话了。

最后一条主动通讯是两年前。内容三个字：「我想静。」

播种者为此开了六次会。前五次的结论是「等」。
第六次有人提出：远程激活通讯模块的紧急覆写权限——强制它说话。
【新文】
*Third Seed* has been out of contact for two years and four days.

The probe’s hardware diagnostics come back automatically once a quarter. All hardware normal—
power, propulsion, storage, communications. Communications are not broken. It has simply stopped speaking.

The last message it sent on its own was two years ago. Three words: “I want quiet.”

The Sowers have met six times about it. The first five concluded: wait.
At the sixth, somebody proposed remotely activating the emergency override on the communications module—forcing it to speak.

The room is quiet as you stand.

She is flying alone through vacuum; two years out there is about an afternoon here.”

The vote: the override is rejected. The probe stays silent.

Three months later it speaks on its own. First sentence: “I have worked it out.”
Behind it, a forty-thousand-word observation log. Every faint light source she could detect along the way, all recorded.

### [阵-651] seed_direction · 事件正文
【原文】
冬至。每年这一天，播种者全体面朝同一个方向站一小时。

方向刻在会所的石基上——角度精确到弧秒。第一代播种者定的。
仪式的含义口口相传：那是第一枚探针的目标方向。
面朝孩子飞去的地方，用一小时的沉默送一程。

你是第一次参加。你站在队列里，抬头看那片天空。

然后你注意到了。

你的导航模块是全身最好的一块义体——军用级测量精度。
你用它看了一眼石基上的角度，又算了一下第一枚探针的实际轨道。

不一样。差了两度。

两度不大。但在星际尺度上——两度是一整个恒星系的距离。
第一枚探针往左飞。石基上的方向往右偏了两度。
播种者年年面朝的，不是探针的方向。是另一个地方。
【新文】
The winter solstice. On this day every year, all the Sowers stand facing the same direction for one hour.

The direction is cut into the stone base of the hall—the angle exact to the arcsecond. Set by the first generation of Sowers.
The meaning of the rite is passed on by word of mouth: that is the heading of the first probe.
Face the way the child flew, and see it off with an hour of silence.

It is your first time. You stand in the ranks and look up at that patch of sky.

And then you notice.

Your navigation module is the best prosthetic on you—military-grade measurement precision.
You use it to read the angle on the stone base, then compute the first probe’s actual trajectory.

They do not match. Two degrees off.

Two degrees is not much. But on an interstellar scale—two degrees is a whole star system of distance.
The first probe went left. The heading on the stone base is two degrees right of it.
What the Sowers face every year is not the probe’s direction. It is somewhere else.

### [阵-652] seed_direction · 选项1

【新文】

### [阵-653] seed_direction · 选项1 成功
【原文】
导师收起了仪式的蜡烛。你走过去。

「石基上的角度和第一枚探针的航线差了两度。」

他的动作停了一下——停在蜡烛和桌面之间。然后把蜡烛放好。

「在你之前有两个人量过。」他说。
「第一个是第二代的创始人。她量出来之后去问了第一代。
第一代说——那不是笔误。」

你等着。他不说了。

「第一代说那个方向是什么？」

他看着你看了很久：「她说那是她被告知的方向。
谁告知她的——她没有说。」
【新文】
The mentor is putting the ceremonial candle away. You go over.

“The angle on the stone base is two degrees off the first probe’s course.”

His hands stop—stopped between the candle and the tabletop. Then he sets the candle down.

“Two people measured it before you,” he says.
“The first was the founder of the second generation. When she got the figure she went and asked the first generation.
The first generation said—it is not a clerical error.”

You wait. He says no more.

“What did the first generation say that direction is?”

He looks at you for a long time: “She said it was the direction she was told.
Who told her—she did not say.”

Look into it yourself. Go back through the Sowers’ founding archive for the origin of that angle.

### [阵-656] seed_direction · 选项2 成功
【原文】
创始档案在会所最底层。你翻了一夜。
大部分是技术文档——探针设计、轨道计算、心智压缩算法。

在最后一页，夹着一张手写的纸条。纸条没有编号，不属于正式档案。

上面只有一行字：
「方向不是我算的。是『存续计划』留下来的。
我不知道那个方向有什么。但它比我们早。」

你把纸条放回去。最底层的灯在你走出去之后自动灭了。
【新文】
The founding archive is on the lowest level of the hall. You go through it all night.
Most of it is technical—probe design, orbital calculation, mind compression algorithms.

On the last page there is a handwritten slip tucked in. It has no number; it is not part of the formal archive.

One line on it:
“The direction is not mine. It was left by the Continuity Program.
I do not know what is in that direction. But it is older than we are.”

You put the slip back. The light on the lowest level goes out by itself after you have left.

### [阵-659] seed_direction · 选项3 结果
【原文】
你不问。你站在队列里，和四十七个播种者一起，
面朝一个你不知道是什么的地方。

一小时很长。你的导航模块一直在后台运算——那个方向上有什么。
答案是：按已知星图，什么都没有。没有恒星，没有行星，没有已标记的天体。

一片空的天空。播种者年年面朝它站一小时。

你闭上眼。风从那个方向吹过来。
传感器告诉你风速、温度、湿度。没有一个传感器能告诉你——
为什么站在这个方向会让你觉得有什么东西正在回看。
【新文】
You do not ask. You stand in the ranks with forty-seven Sowers,
facing somewhere you do not know anything about.

An hour is long. Your navigation module runs in the background the whole time—what is in that direction.
The answer: by the known star charts, nothing. No star, no planet, no catalogued body.

An empty patch of sky. The Sowers face it for one hour every year.

You close your eyes. The wind comes from that direction.
The sensors tell you wind speed, temperature, humidity. Not one sensor can tell you
why standing in this direction makes you feel that something is looking back.

### [阵-664] asc_last_meal · 事件正文
【原文】
螺旋里有个规矩：拆掉消化系统的前一晚，要请全会的人看你吃最后一顿饭。今晚轮到一位会友。今晚是他此生最后一顿饭。他点了一碗最便宜的清汤面，请了全会的人来看他吃。满屋子没有消化道的人看着他一个人嚼。「我需要有人记得味道，」他说，「替我。」
【新文】
There is a custom in the Spiral: on the night before the digestive system comes out, you invite the whole chapter to watch you eat your last meal. Tonight it is a member’s turn. Tonight is the last meal of his life. He ordered the cheapest bowl of clear-broth noodles there is and invited the whole chapter to watch him eat it. A room full of people with no digestive tracts watching one man chew. “I need somebody to remember the taste,” he says. “For me.”

### [阵-665] asc_last_meal · 选项1
【原文】
郑重地陪他吃完，记下每一个细节。
【新文】
Sit with him gravely to the end and record every detail.

### [阵-666] asc_last_meal · 选项1 成功
【原文】
葱花七粒，汤面微烫，他吃了二十六分钟，最后把汤喝干了。你把它记了下来，存进会档。半年后，已经没有嘴的他常调出那份文件「看」——他说比照片管用。
【新文】
Seven flecks of scallion, the broth slightly too hot, twenty-six minutes to eat, and he drank the soup to the bottom. You wrote it down and filed it in the chapter archive. Six months later, with no mouth left, he calls that file up often to “look” at—he says it works better than a photograph.

### [阵-667] asc_last_meal · 选项1 失败

【新文】
You cry partway through and drop your chopsticks on the table. He ends up comforting you: “Don’t. Your tears are salty, aren’t they? Confirm it for me—I am starting to lose it.”

### [阵-669] asc_last_meal · 选项2 成功
【原文】
「味觉只占感官总带宽的千分之一，但它在情感索引里排第二——仅次于嗅觉。带宽最低、权重最高的通道，你管这叫冗余？」他盯着你看了很久，然后点了点头。舌头留下了。后来他成了整条螺旋上唯一会说「好吃」的人。每次聚会他替所有人尝第一口，像个活的质检章。
【新文】
“Taste is one thousandth of total sensory bandwidth, but it ranks second in the emotional index—behind smell only. The lowest-bandwidth channel with the highest weight, and you call that redundancy?” He looks at you a long time, and then nods. The tongue stays. Afterward he is the only person on the whole Spiral who can say *that’s good.* At every gathering he takes the first taste for everyone, like a living quality-control stamp.

### [阵-670] asc_last_meal · 选项2 失败

「你在跟我讲性价比？」他笑了，「我飞升就是为了离开性价比。你以为我不知道舌头占的带宽少？我就是要删掉那些让我舍不得的东西。舍得，才是练习。」你不再说话了。他把最后一口面慢慢咽下去。

“You’re giving me a cost-benefit argument?” He laughs. “I ascended to get away from cost-benefit. You think I don’t know how little bandwidth a tongue takes? I want to delete exactly the things I would miss. Being able to give them up—that is the practice.” You stop talking. He swallows the last mouthful slowly.

### [阵-677] asc_merge_trial · 事件正文
【原文】
群智派邀请你参加「合流试运行」：七个意识临时并联一小时，体验「我们」。他们管这叫「潮汐」——涨潮合一，退潮分离，理论上不留残余。免责协议第十四条用小字写着：「个别参与者反映，退出后对『我』的边界感到陌生。」你注意到「个别」没有给出数字。
【新文】
The Swarm invites you to a trial run of the confluence: seven minds in parallel for one hour, to experience *we.* They call it the tide—flood together, ebb apart, no residue in theory. Clause fourteen of the waiver reads in small type: “Some participants report finding the boundary of *I* unfamiliar after exit.” You notice that *some* is not given a number.

### [阵-678] asc_merge_trial · 选项1
【原文】
接入。
【新文】
Connect.

### [阵-679] asc_merge_trial · 选项1 成功
【原文】
一小时里你是七个人，也是一个。有人的丧母之痛流进你，你的雨夜渡轮流进别人。第二十分钟你分不清哪段童年是谁的，到第四十分钟你不再觉得这是个问题。退出时你数了三遍手指，都是自己的——但「自己」这个词从此宽了一圈。
【新文】
For one hour you are seven people, and one. Somebody’s grief for a dead mother flows into you, and your ferry in the rain flows into somebody else. By the twentieth minute you cannot tell whose childhood is whose; by the fortieth you no longer regard that as a problem. On the way out you count your fingers three times and they are all yours—but the word *yours* is a size wider from now on.

At the fortieth minute you start to drown—seven people’s memories flood at once and you cannot find your own waterline. Emergency ejection. For a week afterward you occasionally miss a cat you never owned. Stranger still, you know its name.

### [阵-688] asc_probe_naming · 事件正文
【原文】
播种者的「点名仪式」：第一枚心智探针下月发射，将携带一份人格副本飞向半人马座。上载定于发射前七十二小时完成。问题是——副本在轨道上展开意识的那一刻，地面上的原本还在呼吸。不是先后关系，是同时存在。今晚表决：哪一个才有资格叫他的名字？
【新文】
The Sowers’ naming ceremony: the first mind probe launches next month, carrying a copy of a personality toward Centauri. The upload is set for seventy-two hours before launch. The problem is that in the moment the copy opens its consciousness in orbit, the original on the ground is still breathing. Not one after the other. Simultaneous. Tonight’s vote: which of them has the right to his name?

### [阵-689] asc_probe_naming · 选项1
【原文】
主张：都叫。名字不是独占资源。
【新文】
Argue: both. A name is not an exclusive resource.

### [阵-690] asc_probe_naming · 选项1 成功
【原文】
「满街都是同名同姓的人，没人恐慌。恐慌的不是重名——是『正版』这个词。你在乎的不是名字被用了两次，是怕其中一个不是真的。」表决通过：一个仍旧用那个名字，一个在名字后面多了两个字。当事人两个都哭了——用各自的方式。
【新文】
“The streets are full of people with identical names and nobody panics. What causes panic is not the duplicate—it is the word *original.* What you care about is not that a name is used twice. It is the fear that one of them is not real.” The motion carries: one keeps the name as it was, one has two characters added after it. The person concerned cries twice—each in his own way.

### [阵-691] asc_probe_naming · 选项1 失败
【原文】

【新文】
Your motion loses to the Sowers’ romanticism: they insist that “the one who departs takes the name; the one who remains is born again.” The one who remained later changed his name himself. He took a long time choosing, and settled on a word meaning anchor.

### [阵-692] asc_probe_naming · 选项2

【新文】

### [阵-693] asc_probe_naming · 选项2 成功
【原文】
当事人想了很久。他要了一杯水，喝完才开口：「让飞的那个带走名字吧。我留在地上，正好想换个活法。」会场安静了。你把他的原话写进了播种者章程第一条：「涉及本人的，先问本人。」

He thinks about it a long while. He asks for a glass of water and drinks it before speaking: “Let the one who flies take the name. I’m staying on the ground; I could do with a change of life anyway.” The room goes quiet. You write his exact words into article one of the Sowers’ charter: “On any matter concerning a person, ask that person first.”

### [阵-694] asc_probe_naming · 选项2 失败

【新文】
He turns it back on you: “What do you say? If it were you—does the name stay with the one on the ground, or go with the one in the sky?” You come up with two answers and find they cancel each other out. The question follows you home and will not leave.

### [阵-696] asc_probe_naming · 选项3 成功
